- **specify-feature**: Detects features from a file, a URL, a repo's roadmap.md, GitHub issues, or a vague prompt, lets the user pick which ones to develop, and scaffolds a numbered specs/ folder with a spec-kit-style requirements.md per feature — evidencing gaps instead of resolving them. Trigger when the user says "specify-feature", "especifica", "define los requirements", or invokes /specify-feature. ([charlstown/carlos-grande-me-docs](https://github.com/charlstown/carlos-grande-me-docs/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/specify-feature.md) · ★1)
- **specify.body**: Every subagent invocation uses the delegation tool with the appropriate `subagent_type` (`architect`, `arch-review`, `planner`). That tool is named `Agent`, in an interactive session and in the headless runtime alike. Do not look for a `Task` tool in your toolbox: `Task` is the tool's former name, still accepted as an alias in `tools` lists and permission rules, and still printed in place of `Agen ([sergeyklay/.agents](https://github.com/sergeyklay/.agents/blob/HEAD/templates/.claude/commands/specify.body.md) · ★5)
- **specify.body**: The request is whatever the user typed after the command name in the message that invoked this skill. If they typed nothing, ask for a tracker reference, a feature description, or the path to an existing spec before starting. ([sergeyklay/.agents](https://github.com/sergeyklay/.agents/blob/HEAD/templates/.copilot/commands/specify.body.md) · ★5)

- **speckit.product-forge.design-system-harvest**: Phase 2 helper: discover the project's EXISTING design system (component library, design tokens, Storybook, Tailwind/CSS config) and emit a read-only manifest that grounds mockups, component decomposition, and verification in real code. - **speckit.product-forge.feature-flag-cleanup**: Cross-cutting stale-flag audit. Reads every feature's `.forge-status.yml` (via the Path-Resolution Contract's `enumerate()`, so flat and domain-nested layouts both work) plus optional `tasks.md`, and produces a consolidated report covering feature status, file/module conflicts between in-flight features, a dependency graph, and a suggested merge order. - **speckit.product-forge.portfolio**: Cross-cutting portfolio view over every feature managed by Product Forge. Reads `features/*/.forge-status.yml` (and optional `tasks.md`) and produces a consolidated report covering feature status, file/module conflicts between in-flight features, a dependency graph, and a suggested merge order. Use: "show portfolio", "feature portfolio", "what's blocked", "/speckit.product-forge.portfolio" ([B0yZ4kr14/TSiSIP](https://github.com/B0yZ4kr14/TSiSIP/blob/HEAD/.specify/extensions/product-forge/commands/portfolio.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.pre-impl-review**: Phase 5C: Combined design review, architecture review, and risk assessment gate before writing any code. Loads wireframes, UX patterns, plan architecture, and codebase analysis to produce a structured review with risk register. Optional for features with &lt;=5 tasks and no UI. Use: "pre-impl review", "review before coding", "/speckit.product-forge.pre-impl-review" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/pre-impl-review.md) · ★23 · 2 copies)
- **speckit.product-forge.problem-discovery**: Phase 0: Validates the problem before any research begins. Use before research: "discover the problem", "/speckit.product-forge.problem-discovery" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/problem-discovery.md) · ★23 · 2 copies)
- **speckit.product-forge.product-spec**: Phase 2: Interactive product specification creation. Asks detailed questions about desired level of detail and document decomposition. Creates product-spec.md, structured journeys (journeys/journeys.yml as the E2E source of truth), wireframes, metrics.md, and design-system-grounded clickable mockups with a component map. All documents are cross-linked via product-spec/README.md index. Use with: "create product spec", "/speckit.product-forge.product-spec" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/product-spec.md) · ★23)
- **speckit.product-forge.product-spec**: Phase 2: Interactive product specification creation. Asks detailed questions about desired level of detail and document decomposition. Creates product-spec.md, user-journey.md, wireframes, metrics.md, and optional mockups.html. All documents are cross-linked via product-spec/README.md index. Use with: "create product spec", "/speckit.product-forge.product-spec" ([B0yZ4kr14/TSiSIP](https://github.com/B0yZ4kr14/TSiSIP/blob/HEAD/.specify/extensions/product-forge/commands/product-spec.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.release-readiness**: Phase 9: Pre-ship readiness checklist. Covers feature flags, rollout strategy, rollback plan, documentation, monitoring, analytics, and deployment dependencies. Consolidates api-docs, tracking-plan, and security-check status into one gate. Optional for internal/backend-only features. Use: "release readiness", "ready to ship?", "/speckit.product-forge.release-readiness" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/release-readiness.md) · ★23 · 2 copies)
- **speckit.product-forge.research**: Phase 1: Adaptive multi-dimensional feature research. Assesses input richness and auto-adjusts interview depth — minimal input triggers full 7-question interview, rich input skips to confirmation. Mandatory: competitors, UX/UI, codebase. Optional: tech stack, metrics/ROI. Saves to features/&lt;name&gt;/research/. Use with: "research feature", "/speckit.product-forge.research" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/research.md) · ★23 · 2 copies)
- **speckit.product-forge.retrospective**: Post-launch retrospective comparing predicted metrics from research/metrics-roi.md against real data pulled from connected MCPs (PostHog / Amplitude product analytics, Sentry error tracking, NewRelic APM) or manual input. Closes the loop on the full product lifecycle. Run 2+ weeks after shipping. Use: "retrospective", "/speckit.product-forge.retrospective {feature-slug}" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/retrospective.md) · ★23)
- **speckit.product-forge.retrospective**: Post-launch retrospective comparing predicted metrics from research/metrics-roi.md against real data from NewRelic, Analytics MCP, or manual input. Closes the loop on the full product lifecycle. Run 2+ weeks after shipping. Use: "retrospective", "/speckit.product-forge.retrospective {feature-slug}" ([B0yZ4kr14/TSiSIP](https://github.com/B0yZ4kr14/TSiSIP/blob/HEAD/.specify/extensions/product-forge/commands/retrospective.md))
- **speckit.product-forge.revalidate**: Phase 3: Iterative revalidation and correction cycle. Shows product spec summary, collects user feedback in chat, applies corrections via a dedicated agent, and loops until explicit user approval. All revision history recorded in review.md. Use with: "revalidate spec", "review product spec", "/speckit.product-forge.revalidate" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/revalidate.md) · ★23 · 2 copies)
- **speckit.product-forge.security-check**: Feature-specific OWASP security audit. Reads plan.md to detect auth, payments, user input, and file handling — then checks only the relevant attack vectors. Generates a prioritized fix list with code-level evidence. Run after implement, before ship. Use: "security check", "/speckit.product-forge.security-check" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/security-check.md) · ★23 · 2 copies)
- **speckit.product-forge.spec-merge**: Cross-cutting (living spec): merge a completed feature's delta specs (ADDED/MODIFIED/REMOVED requirements) into the canonical specs/ source of truth, then archive the change folder with audit history. Makes the spec spec-anchored — a living source of truth across the feature's lifetime, not a per-change artifact. Use with: "merge spec", "archive change", "/speckit.product-forge.spec-merge" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/spec-merge.md) · ★23)
- **speckit.product-forge.status**: Show the current Product Forge lifecycle status for a feature. Reads .forge-status.yml, displays all phases with completion state, lists available artifacts, and recommends the next action. Works for any feature in the features/ directory. Use with: "forge status", "show status", "/speckit.product-forge.status" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/status.md) · ★23 · 2 copies)
- **speckit.product-forge.sync-verify**: Cross-cutting artifact consistency checker. Verifies alignment across ALL lifecycle layers — research ↔ product-spec ↔ spec.md ↔ plan.md ↔ tasks.md ↔ code. Detects forward drift (earlier artifacts not reflected in later) and backward drift (later decisions that should update earlier artifacts). Runnable between ANY phases. Human-in-the-loop for every CRITICAL/WARNING resolution. Use: "sync-verify", "check consistency", "/speckit.product-forge.sync-verify" ([VaiYav/speckit-product-forge](https://github.com/VaiYav/speckit-product-forge/blob/HEAD/commands/sync-verify.md) · ★23 · 2 copies)
- **speckit.product-forge.tasks**: Phase 5B: Generate task breakdown from plan.md with cross-validation against product-spec. - **speckit.product-forge.test-plan**: Phase 8A: Creates a comprehensive test plan from feature artifacts. - **speckit.product-forge.test-run**: Phase 8B: Executes test cases via playwright-cli (interactive browser agent), tracks bugs in bugs/&lt;BUG-NNN&gt;.md, auto-fixes P0/P1 bugs and retests, performs gap analysis when bugs require spec changes. - **spx:assess**: Pre-specify idea pipeline. Run one of five phases — intake, research, define, shape, decide — under .specify/assessments/&lt;slug&gt;/. ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/assess.md))
- **spx:bug**: Artifact-based bugfix loop. Run one of three phases — assess, fix, test — under .specify/bugs/&lt;slug&gt;/. Assess delegates to the investigate machinery (root-cause + Tidewave capture); only the fix phase edits source; test verifies the fix holds without regressions. ([carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir](https://github.com/carlosgr092/claude-speckit-phx-elixir/blob/HEAD/commands/bug.md))

- **spx:clarify**: Identify underspecified areas in the current feature spec by asking up to 5 highly targeted clarification questions, scored across coverage dimensions, and encoding the answers back into the spec. - **spx:constitution**: Create or update the project constitution (the canonical Iron-Law corpus) from interactive or provided principle inputs, regenerating every derived enforcement copy and keeping dependent templates in sync.
